		TextBox_d.TextSize = 14
		TextBox_d.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		TextBox_d.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(27, 27, 27)
		TextBox_d.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
		TextBox_d.PlaceholderText = "Ask dex Assist..."
		TextBox_d.Size = UDim2.new(0, 642, 0, 22)
		TextBox_d.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 352)
		TextBox_d.Text = ""
		TextBox_d.Parent = Frame_2

		local UICorner_e = Instance.new("UICorner")
		UICorner_e.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
		UICorner_e.Parent = TextBox_d

		-- CHAT SYSTEM
		local nextY = 158 -- after welcome message (50 + 88 + 20 padding)
		local isProcessing = false
		local chatHistory = {} -- keeps context between messages

		local function measureText(text, fontSize, font, maxWidth)
			local defaultFont = Enum.Font.SourceSans
			if font == "Code" or font == Enum.Font.Code then
				defaultFont = Enum.Font.Code
			end
			local ok, size = pcall(function()
				return TextService:GetTextSize(text or "", fontSize or 13, defaultFont, Vector2.new(maxWidth or 560, 100000))
			end)
			if ok and size then
				return math.ceil(size.X), math.ceil(size.Y)
			end
			local lines = 1
			local maxLen = 0
			for l in (text or ""):gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
				lines += 1
				if #l > maxLen then maxLen = #l end
			end
			return math.min(maxWidth or 560, maxLen * 7), lines * 18
		end

		local function createAgentLabel(parent, posY)
			local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			lbl.BorderSizePixel = 0
			lbl.TextSize = 28
			lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
			lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
			lbl.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
			lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 242)
			lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 24)
			lbl.Text = "Agent"
			lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 582, 0, posY)
			lbl.Parent = parent
			return lbl
		end

		local function createUserBubble(parent, text, posY)
			local maxTextWidth = 440
			local padX, padY = 12, 8
			local textW, textH = measureText(text, 13, Enum.Font.SourceSans, maxTextWidth)
			local bubbleW = math.clamp(textW + padX * 2, 60, maxTextWidth + padX * 2)
			local bubbleH = textH + padY * 2
			local posX = math.max(10, 644 - bubbleW - 14)

			local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
			frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(67, 67, 67)
			frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, bubbleW, 0, bubbleH)
			frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, posX, 0, posY)
			frame.ClipsDescendants = false
			frame.Parent = parent

			Instance.new("UICorner", frame)

			local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			label.TextWrapped = true
			label.BorderSizePixel = 0
			label.TextSize = 13
			label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
			label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
			label.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
			label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 242)
			label.Size = UDim2.new(0, textW, 0, textH)
			label.Position = UDim2.new(0, padX, 0, padY)
			label.Text = text
			label.Parent = frame

			return frame, bubbleH
		end

		local function createAILogo(parent, posY)
			local logo = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
			logo.BorderSizePixel = 0
			logo.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			logo.Image = "rbxassetid://82562144286715"
			logo.Size = UDim2.new(0, 146, 0, 28)
			logo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			logo.Position = UDim2.new(0, -18, 0, posY)
			logo.Parent = parent
			return logo
		end

		local function createAIBubble(parent, posY, text)
			local maxTextWidth = 560
			local padX, padY = 12, 10
			local hasText = text and text ~= ""

			local textW, textH = 80, 16
			if hasText then
				textW, textH = measureText(text, 13, Enum.Font.SourceSans, maxTextWidth)
			end

			local bubbleW = hasText and math.clamp(textW + padX * 2, 60, maxTextWidth + padX * 2) or 110
			local bubbleH = textH + padY * 2 + 2

			local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
			frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(67, 67, 67)
			frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, bubbleW, 0, bubbleH)
			frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, posY)
			frame.ClipsDescendants = false
			frame.Parent = parent

			Instance.new("UICorner", frame)

			local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			label.TextWrapped = true
			label.BorderSizePixel = 0
			label.TextSize = 13
			label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
			label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
			label.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
			label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 242)
			label.Size = UDim2.new(0, textW, 0, textH + 2)
			label.Position = UDim2.new(0, padX, 0, padY)
			label.Text = ""
			label.Parent = frame

			return frame, label, bubbleH
		end

		-- Code/script bubble: dark terminal-style block with a Copy button, matching
		-- the LMG converter aesthetic (mono font, green text, blue copy button).
		local function createCodeBubble(parent, code, posY)
			code = code:gsub("^%s*\n", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")

			local lineCount = 1
			for _ in code:gmatch("\n") do
				lineCount += 1
			end

			local lineHeight = 16
			local padding = 10
			local buttonHeight = 24
			local contentHeight = lineCount * lineHeight + 12

			local codeWidth = 580
			local scrollWidth = codeWidth - (padding * 2)
			local scrollHeight = math.clamp(contentHeight, 48, 280)
			local frameHeight = padding + scrollHeight + padding + buttonHeight + padding

			local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
			frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 37, 36)
			frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, codeWidth, 0, frameHeight)
			frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, posY)
			frame.ClipsDescendants = false
			frame.Parent = parent

			Instance.new("UICorner", frame)

			local maxLineWidth = scrollWidth - 16
			for line in code:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
				local okBounds, bounds = pcall(function()
					return TextService:GetTextSize(line, 13, Enum.Font.Code, Vector2.new(100000, 20))
				end)
				if okBounds and bounds and bounds.X > maxLineWidth then
					maxLineWidth = math.ceil(bounds.X)
				end
			end

			local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
			scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
			scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			scroll.Size = UDim2.new(0, scrollWidth, 0, scrollHeight)
			scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, padding, 0, padding)
			scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4
			scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, maxLineWidth + 24, 0, contentHeight)
			scroll.ClipsDescendants = true
			scroll.Parent = frame

			local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			label.BorderSizePixel = 0
			label.TextSize = 13
			label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
			label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			label.Font = Enum.Font.Code
			label.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Inconsolata.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
			label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(191, 243, 191)
			label.Size = UDim2.new(0, maxLineWidth + 24, 0, contentHeight)
			label.Text = code
			label.Parent = scroll

			local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
			button.BorderSizePixel = 0
			button.TextSize = 14
			button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(1, 135, 198)
			button.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
			button.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
			button.Size = UDim2.new(0, scrollWidth, 0, buttonHeight)
			button.Position = UDim2.new(0, padding, 0, padding + scrollHeight + padding)
			button.Text = "Copy Script"
			button.Parent = frame

			Instance.new("UICorner", button)

			button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local ok = pcall(function()
					if setclipboard then
						setclipboard(code)
					elseif toclipboard then
						toclipboard(code)
					end
				end)
				button.Text = ok and "Copied!" or "Copy failed"
				task.delay(1.2, function()
					if button and button.Parent then
						button.Text = "Copy Script"
					end
				end)
			end)

			return frame, frameHeight
		end

		-- Splits an AI reply into plain-text segments and ```code``` segments so each
		-- can be rendered with its own bubble in the order they appear.
		local function parseSegments(text)
			local segments = {}
			local pos = 1
			while true do
				local codeStart, codeEnd, code = text:find("```%a*\n?(.-)```", pos)
				if not codeStart then
					local rest = text:sub(pos)
					rest = rest:gsub("^%s*\n", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
					if rest ~= "" then
						table.insert(segments, { kind = "text", content = rest })
					end
					break
				end

				local before = text:sub(pos, codeStart - 1)
				before = before:gsub("^%s*\n", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
				if before ~= "" then
					table.insert(segments, { kind = "text", content = before })
				end

				table.insert(segments, { kind = "code", content = code })
				pos = codeEnd + 1
			end
			return segments
		end

		-- ===== AI BACKEND (Groq - OpenAI-compatible chat completions) =====

		local function getInstancePath(inst)
			local parts = {}
			local current = inst
			local guard = 0
			while current and current ~= game and guard < 64 do
				table.insert(parts, 1, current.Name)
				local ok, parent = pcall(function() return current.Parent end)
				if not ok then break end
				current = parent
				guard += 1
			end
			return "game." .. table.concat(parts, ".")
		end
		local getPath = getInstancePath

		local function findGameInstances(query, maxResults)
			maxResults = maxResults or 8
			local results = {}
			local lowerQuery = string.lower(query or "")

			pcall(function()
				for _, inst in ipairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
					if #results >= maxResults then break end

					local okName, name = pcall(function() return inst.Name end)
					if okName and (lowerQuery == "" or string.find(string.lower(name), lowerQuery, 1, true)) then
						local childNames = {}
						local okChildren, children = pcall(function() return inst:GetChildren() end)
						if okChildren then
							for i, child in ipairs(children) do
								if i > 8 then break end
								table.insert(childNames, child.Name .. " (" .. child.ClassName .. ")")
							end
						end

						table.insert(results, {
							Name = name,
							ClassName = inst.ClassName,
							Path = getInstancePath(inst),
							ChildCount = okChildren and #children or 0,
							Children = childNames,
						})
					end
				end
			end)

			return results
		end

		local function findScriptByQuery(query)
			local lowerQuery = string.lower(query or "")
			local best = nil

			pcall(function()
				for _, inst in ipairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
					if inst:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") then
						local okName, name = pcall(function() return inst.Name end)
						if okName and string.find(string.lower(name), lowerQuery, 1, true) then
							best = inst
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			return best
		end

		local function decompileScriptSource(scr)
			local ok, source = pcall(env.decompile or function() end, scr)
			if ok and source and source:gsub("%s+", "") ~= "" then
				return source
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function buildToolsSchema()
			return {
				{
					type = "function",
					["function"] = {
						name = "find_game_instance",
						description = "Search the current Roblox game's live instance tree (the same tree shown in the Explorer window) for objects whose name contains the given text. Returns matching instances with their class name, full path, child count, and a preview of their children. Use this whenever the user asks what an object, script, service, or instance is, what it does, or where to find it in the game -- look it up instead of guessing.",
						parameters = {
							type = "object",
							properties = {
								query = {
									type = "string",
									description = "Text to search for in instance names (case-insensitive, partial match)."
								}
							},
							required = {"query"}
						}
					}
				},
				{
					type = "function",
					["function"] = {
						name = "read_script_source",
						description = "Find a script (Script, LocalScript, or ModuleScript) in the game by name and decompile/read its actual source code, the same way the Script Viewer app does. Use this whenever the user asks what a specific script does, asks you to explain or analyze its code, or wants a summary of its logic. The source may be truncated if very long.",
						parameters = {
							type = "object",
							properties = {
								query = {
									type = "string",
									description = "Name (or partial name) of the script to find and read."
								}
							},
							required = {"query"}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		end

		local function runTool(name, argsJson)
			local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
			local ok, args = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(argsJson) end)
			if not ok then args = {} end

			if name == "find_game_instance" then
				local results = findGameInstances(args.query or "")
				if #results == 0 then
					return "No instances found matching '" .. tostring(args.query) .. "'."
				end
				local okJson, json = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONEncode(results) end)
				if okJson then return json end
				return "Error searching instances."
			elseif name == "read_script_source" then
				local scr = findScriptByQuery(args.query or "")
				if not scr then
					return "No script found matching '" .. tostring(args.query) .. "'."
				end

				local source = decompileScriptSource(scr)
				if not source then
					return "Found the script at " .. getPath(scr) .. " (" .. scr.ClassName .. ") but could not decompile it. It may be a server-side script, or your executor may not support decompiling it."
				end

				local truncated = false
				if #source > 6000 then
					source = source:sub(1, 6000)
					truncated = true
				end

				local header = "Path: " .. getPath(scr) .. "\nClassName: " .. scr.ClassName .. "\n\n"
				if truncated then
					header = header .. "-- NOTE: source truncated to 6000 characters --\n\n"
				end

				return header .. source
			end

			return "Unknown tool: " .. tostring(name)
		end

		local function httpPostJson(url, headers, bodyTable)
			local reqFunc = (syn and syn.request) or request or http_request
			if not reqFunc then
				return nil
			end

			local jsonBody = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(bodyTable)

			local ok, res = pcall(function()
				return reqFunc({
					Url = url,
					Method = "POST",
					Headers = headers,
					Body = jsonBody
				})
			end)

			if ok and res and res.Body and res.Body ~= "" then
				return res.Body
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function askDexAI(prompt)
			if not GROQ_API_KEY or GROQ_API_KEY == "" or GROQ_API_KEY == "COLOQUE_SUA_CHAVE_AQUI" then
				return nil, "missing_key"
			end

			-- keep only the last few exchanges so requests stay fast as the chat grows
			while #chatHistory > 8 do
				table.remove(chatHistory, 1)
			end

			local messages = {
				{ role = "system", content = "You are Dex AI, a helpful assistant built into a Roblox exploit debugging tool called Dex. You have access to two tools: find_game_instance, which searches the live game's instance tree (the same tree shown in the Explorer window), and read_script_source, which decompiles and reads the actual source code of a script (the same way the Script Viewer app does). Use find_game_instance whenever the user asks about an object, service, or instance, and use read_script_source whenever the user asks what a script does or wants its code explained -- look things up instead of guessing. Keep answers concise and useful. You will NEED to make scripts to help the user learn coding and others."}
			}
			for _, entry in ipairs(chatHistory) do
				table.insert(messages, entry)
			end
			table.insert(messages, { role = "user", content = prompt })

			local headers = {
				["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
				["Authorization"] = "Bearer " .. GROQ_API_KEY
			}

			local tools = buildToolsSchema()

			for round = 1, 3 do
				local body = {
					model = GROQ_MODEL,
					messages = messages,
					temperature = 0.5,
					tools = tools,
					tool_choice = "auto"
				}

				local ok, raw = pcall(httpPostJson, "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions", headers, body)
				if not (ok and raw) then
					return nil, "network_error"
				end

				local decodeOk, decoded = pcall(function()
					return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(raw)
				end)

				if not (decodeOk and decoded) then
					return nil, "parse_error"
				end

				if decoded.error then
					return nil, decoded.error.message or "api_error"
				end

				local choice = decoded.choices and decoded.choices[1]
				if not choice or not choice.message then
					return nil, "parse_error"
				end

				local message = choice.message

				if message.tool_calls and #message.tool_calls > 0 then
					table.insert(messages, message)

					for _, call in ipairs(message.tool_calls) do
						local result = runTool(call["function"].name, call["function"].arguments)
						table.insert(messages, {
							role = "tool",
							tool_call_id = call.id,
							content = result
						})
					end
					-- loop again so the model can read the tool results and respond
				else
					local content = message.content or ""
					table.insert(chatHistory, { role = "user", content = prompt })
					table.insert(chatHistory, { role = "assistant", content = content })
					return content
				end
			end

			return nil, "tool_loop_exceeded"
		end

		local function typewriterEffect(label, fullText, maxDuration)
			label.Text = ""
			local len = #fullText
			if len == 0 then return end

			maxDuration = maxDuration or 1.2
			local perChar = math.min(0.015, maxDuration / len)

			-- for long replies, update in small chunks instead of one char at a time
			local step = math.max(1, math.floor(len / (maxDuration / 0.03)))

			local i = 0
			while i < len do
				i = math.min(len, i + step)
				label.Text = string.sub(fullText, 1, i)
				task.wait(perChar * step)
			end
		end

		TextBox_d.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
			if enterPressed and not isProcessing then
				local msg = TextBox_d.Text
				if msg and msg:gsub("%s+", "") ~= "" then
					isProcessing = true
					TextBox_d.Text = ""

					-- Agent label
					createAgentLabel(ScrollingFrame_3, nextY)
					nextY += 28

					-- User bubble
					local userFrame, userHeight = createUserBubble(ScrollingFrame_3, msg, nextY)
					nextY += userHeight + 18

					-- AI Logo above AI message
					createAILogo(ScrollingFrame_3, nextY)
					nextY += 28 + 8

					-- Temporary "thinking" bubble while we wait for the reply
					local thinkFrame, thinkLabel, thinkHeight = createAIBubble(ScrollingFrame_3, nextY, "thinking...")

					task.spawn(function()
						local thinking = true
						task.spawn(function()
							local dots = {".", "..", "..."}
							local i = 1
							while thinking do
								thinkLabel.Text = "thinking" .. dots[i]
								i = (i % 3) + 1
								task.wait(0.35)
							end
						end)

						local reply, errReason = askDexAI(msg)
						thinking = false
						thinkFrame:Destroy()

						if not reply then
							if errReason == "missing_key" then
								reply = "No API key set. Open the DexAssist module and set GROQ_API_KEY (get a free one at console.groq.com/keys)."
							else
								reply = "Sorry, I couldn't reach Dex AI right now. Check your internet connection or try again in a moment."
							end
						end

						local segments = parseSegments(reply)
						local curY = nextY

						for _, segment in ipairs(segments) do
							if segment.kind == "code" then
								-- Espaçamento extra antes e depois do bloco de código
								curY += 4
								local codeFrame, codeHeight = createCodeBubble(ScrollingFrame_3, segment.content, curY)
								curY += codeHeight + 8
							else
								-- Frame da mensagem com o tamanho exato do texto gerado
								local textFrame, textLabel, textHeight = createAIBubble(ScrollingFrame_3, curY, segment.content)
								typewriterEffect(textLabel, segment.content, 1.2)
								curY += textHeight + 6
							end

							-- keep the view following along as bubbles are added
							ScrollingFrame_3.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, curY + 60)
							ScrollingFrame_3.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, curY - 336 + 60))
						end

						nextY = curY + 8

						-- Final scroll update
						ScrollingFrame_3.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, nextY + 60)
						ScrollingFrame_3.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, nextY - 336 + 60))

						isProcessing = false
					end)
				end
			end
		end)

		DexAssist.Window = window
	end

	return DexAssist
end
return {InitDeps = InitDeps, InitAfterMain = InitAfterMain, Main = MainFunc}
end,

["Reconstructor"] = function()
--[[
	Script Reconstructor App Module

	Scans decompilable client-visible scripts (ReplicatedStorage, etc) for
	references to ServerStorage / ServerScriptService paths, and attempts to
	reconstruct the referenced instances/scripts inside their real location
	in the Explorer (ServerStorage items land in ServerStorage, ServerScriptService
	items land in ServerScriptService).

	Original scanning logic by alurion / Project Syn team (.gg/4KYxV25nqu),
	adapted to run inside Dex as a proper App.
]]
local Main, Lib, Apps, Settings

local function InitDeps(Data)
	Main, Lib, Apps, Settings = Data.Main, Data.Lib, Data.Apps, Data.Settings
	env, service, plr = Data.env, Data.service, Data.plr
end

local function InitAfterMain()
end

local function MainFunc()
	local Reconstructor = {}

	-- Runs headless during the intro (no window, no app, no button).
	Reconstructor.OnLog = nil

	local logCount = 0
	local function guiLog(text)
		print("[YSS SCANNER]: " .. text)
		logCount = logCount + 1
		if Reconstructor.OnLog then
			pcall(Reconstructor.OnLog, text, logCount)
		end
	end
		local header = "--[[ Reconstructed by Dex Script Reconstructor - original scanning logic by alurion / Project Syn team ]]"

		local allowed_roots = {
			server_storage = true,
			server_script_service = true,
		}

		local function is_folder(name)
			return name:sub(-1):lower() == "s"
		end

		local function detect_script_type(source)
			if source:find("return%s+") then
				return "ModuleScript"
			end
			return "Script"
		end

		local function extract_function(source, func_name)
			local pattern = "function%s+" .. func_name .. "%s*%b()%s*(.-)\nend"
			local body = string.match(source, pattern)

			if body then
				return "function " .. func_name .. "()\n" .. body .. "\nend"
			end

			local pattern2 = func_name .. "%s*=%s*function%s*%b()%s*(.-)\nend"
			local body2 = string.match(source, pattern2)

			if body2 then
				return "function " .. func_name .. "()\n" .. body2 .. "\nend"
			end

			return nil
		end

		local function get_instance_type(name, index, total_parts, source_code)
			local is_last = (index == total_parts)

			if is_folder(name) then
				return "Folder"
			end

			if is_last then
				return detect_script_type(source_code)
			end

			return "Folder"
		end

		local function recreate_path(path_string, source_name, source_code)
			local parts = string.split(path_string, ".")
			if #parts < 2 then return false end

			local root_name = parts[1]
			if not allowed_roots[root_name] then return false end

			local rootServiceName = (root_name == "server_storage") and "ServerStorage" or "ServerScriptService"
			local root = game:FindFirstChild(rootServiceName)
			if not root then return false end

			local current = root
			local created = {}

			for i = 2, #parts do
				local name = parts[i]
				local instance_type = get_instance_type(name, i, #parts, source_code)

				local existing = current:FindFirstChild(name)

				if not existing then
					local ok, new_inst = pcall(Instance.new, instance_type)
					if not ok or not new_inst then return false end
					new_inst.Name = name

					if instance_type == "ModuleScript" then
						local func_name = parts[#parts]
						local extracted = extract_function(source_code, func_name)

						if extracted then
							new_inst.Source =
								header .. "\n\nlocal module = {}\n\n" ..
								extracted .. "\n\nreturn module"
						else
							new_inst.Source =
								header .. "\n\nlocal module = {}\n\n-- fallback\n" ..
								source_code .. "\n\nreturn module"
						end

					elseif instance_type == "Script" then
						new_inst.Source =
							header .. "\n\n-- reconstructed script\n" ..
							source_code
					end

					pcall(function() new_inst.Parent = current end)
					table.insert(created, new_inst.Name .. " [" .. instance_type .. "]")
					current = new_inst
				else
					current = existing
				end
			end

			if #created > 0 then
				guiLog("Created: " .. table.concat(created, " -> "))
				guiLog("   from: " .. source_name)
			end

			return true
		end

		local function scan_source(source, source_name)
			local service_variables = {}

			for var_name in string.gmatch(source, 'local%s+([%w_]+)%s*=%s*game:GetService%("([%w_]+)"%)') do
				if source:find('ServerStorage') then
					service_variables[var_name] = "server_storage"
				elseif source:find('ServerScriptService') then
					service_variables[var_name] = "server_script_service"
				end
			end

			for var_name, service_name in pairs(service_variables) do
				for path in string.gmatch(source, var_name .. "%.([%w_%.]+)") do
					recreate_path(service_name .. "." .. path, source_name, source)
				end
			end

			for path in string.gmatch(source, "ServerStorage%.([%w_%.]+)") do
				recreate_path("server_storage." .. path, source_name, source)
			end

			for path in string.gmatch(source, "ServerScriptService%.([%w_%.]+)") do
				recreate_path("server_script_service." .. path, source_name, source)
			end
		end

		-- Fast mode: hard time budget + script cap so the intro never stalls.
		local SCAN_TIME_BUDGET = 3
		local SCAN_SCRIPT_LIMIT = 40
		local scanDeadline, scannedScripts = 0, 0
		local scannedSources = {}

		local function budgetExceeded()
			return os.clock() > scanDeadline or scannedScripts >= SCAN_SCRIPT_LIMIT
		end

		local function scan_container(container)
			if budgetExceeded() then return end
			local ok, descendants = pcall(container.GetDescendants, container)
			if not ok then return end
			local yieldCounter = 0
			for _, obj in ipairs(descendants) do
				if budgetExceeded() then return end
				if obj:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") then
					local decompileFunc = env.decompile or decompile
					if decompileFunc then
						scannedScripts = scannedScripts + 1
						local success, result = pcall(decompileFunc, obj)
						if success and type(result) == "string" and #result > 0 then
							-- Skip duplicate sources (same module cloned many times).
							local key = #result .. ":" .. result:sub(1, 120)
							if not scannedSources[key] then
								scannedSources[key] = true
								if result:find("ServerStorage") or result:find("ServerScriptService") then
									scan_source(result, obj:GetFullName())
								end
							end
						end
						yieldCounter = yieldCounter + 1
						if yieldCounter >= 25 then
							yieldCounter = 0
							task.wait()
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

	Reconstructor.Running = false
	Reconstructor.Run = function(onLog)
		if Reconstructor.Running then return end
		Reconstructor.Running = true
		Reconstructor.OnLog = onLog

		guiLog("YSS Reconstruction Scan Starting...")

		scanDeadline = os.clock() + SCAN_TIME_BUDGET
		scannedScripts = 0
		scannedSources = {}

		pcall(game.GetService, game, "ServerStorage")
		pcall(game.GetService, game, "ServerScriptService")

		-- Only the containers that actually replicate scripts to the client.
		local targets = {"ReplicatedStorage", "ReplicatedFirst", "StarterPlayer", "StarterGui", "StarterPack", "Workspace", "Lighting"}
		for _, name in ipairs(targets) do
			if budgetExceeded() then break end
			local container = game:FindFirstChild(name)
			if container then
				guiLog("Scanning " .. name .. "...")
				pcall(scan_container, container)
			end
		end

		guiLog("Reconstruction Scan Complete.")
		Reconstructor.Running = false
	end

	-- Kept for compatibility; the scan now runs automatically from the intro.
	Reconstructor.Init = function() end

	return Reconstructor
end
return {InitDeps = InitDeps, InitAfterMain = InitAfterMain, Main = MainFunc}
end,

["DebugExplorer"] = function()
--[[
	DataStore Editor App Module

	Agency's powerful utility allowing to access and mess with game's DataStore.
]]
local Main, Lib, Apps, Settings
local Explorer, Properties, ScriptViewer, Notebook
local API, RMD, env, service, plr, create, createSimple

local function InitDeps(Data)
	Main, Lib, Apps, Settings = Data.Main, Data.Lib, Data.Apps, Data.Settings
	API, RMD, env, service, plr = Data.API, Data.RMD, Data.env, Data.service, Data.plr
	create, createSimple = Data.create, Data.createSimple
end

local function InitAfterMain()
	Explorer = Apps.Explorer
	Properties = Apps.Properties
	ScriptViewer = Apps.ScriptViewer
	Notebook = Apps.Notebook
end

local function MainFunc()
	local DebugExplorer = {}

	local window

	-- ── Compila dados do player ──────────────────────
	local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local function compilePlayerData(player)
		local data = {
			Inventory = {},
			Values    = {},
		}
		for _, child in ipairs(player:GetDescendants()) do
			if child:IsA("StringValue") or
			   child:IsA("NumberValue") or
			   child:IsA("IntValue")    or
			   child:IsA("BoolValue")   then
				data.Values[child.Name] = {
					Type  = child.ClassName,
					Value = child.Value,
				}
			end
		end
		local inventory = player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if inventory then
			for _, item in ipairs(inventory:GetChildren()) do
				table.insert(data.Inventory, item.Name)
			end
		end
		return data
	end

	-- ── Edit Value ──────────────────────
	local ValEditorWindow = false

	local function DisplayValueEditor(Player)
		if ValEditorWindow == true then return end
		ValEditorWindow = true

		local win = Lib.Window.new()
		win.Alignable = false
		win.Resizable = false
		win:SetTitle("Edit Value")
		win:SetSize(260, 130)
		win.GuiElems.Close.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
			ValEditorWindow = false
		end)

		local nameLabel = Lib.Label.new()
		nameLabel.Text     = "Value: "
		nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 13, 0, 30)
		nameLabel.Size     = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 20)
		win:Add(nameLabel)

		local nameBox = Lib.ViewportTextBox.new()
		nameBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 55, 0, 30)
		nameBox.Size     = UDim2.new(0, 190, 0, 20)
		win:Add(nameBox, "NameBox")

		local saveButton = Lib.Button.new()

		nameBox.TextBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			saveButton:SetDisabled(#nameBox:GetText() == 0)
		end)

		local errorLabel = Lib.Label.new()
		errorLabel.Text       = ""
		errorLabel.Position   = UDim2.new(0, 5, 1, -45)
		errorLabel.Size       = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 20)
		errorLabel.TextColor3 = Settings.Theme.Important
		win:Add(errorLabel, "Error")

		local cancelButton = Lib.Button.new()
		cancelButton.Text     = "Cancel"
		cancelButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -120, 1, -25)
		cancelButton.Size     = UDim2.new(0, 115, 0, 20)
		cancelButton.OnClick:Connect(function()
			ValEditorWindow = false
			win:Close()
		end)
		win:Add(cancelButton)

		saveButton.Text     = "Save"
		saveButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 1, -25)
		saveButton.Size     = UDim2.new(0, 117, 0, 20)
		saveButton.OnClick:Connect(function()
			local rawText = nameBox:GetText()

			if rawText:sub(1, 3) == "RBX" then
				errorLabel.Text = "Error: Name begins with 'RBX'"
				return
			end

			local success, parsed = pcall(function()
				return HttpService:JSONDecode(rawText)
			end)

			if success and typeof(parsed) == "table" then
				local PlayerData = {}
				local result = pcall(function()
					PlayerData[Player.Name] = parsed
					return true
				end)
				if result == "Success" then
					print("Data applied to server")
				else
					warn("Server error: " .. tostring(result))
				end
			else
				errorLabel.Text = "Error: Invalid JSON format"
			end

			ValEditorWindow = false
			win:Close()
		end)
		win:Add(saveButton, "SaveButton")

		win:SetTitle("Edit Value")
		win.Elements.Error.Text = ""

		local compiled = compilePlayerData(Player)
		local json     = HttpService:JSONEncode(compiled)
		win.Elements.NameBox:SetText(tostring(json))
		win.Elements.SaveButton:SetDisabled(true)
		win:Show()
	end

	DebugExplorer.Init = function()
		-- ── Window principal (DataStore Editor) ───────────────────────────
		window = Lib.Window.new()
		window:SetTitle("DataStore Editor")
		window:Resize(205, 530)
		DebugExplorer.Window = window

		local content = window.GuiElems.Content

		-- ── GUI do Packages ───────────────────────────────────
		local List             = Instance.new("Frame")
		local PlayerData       = Instance.new("TextButton")
		local Indent           = Instance.new("Frame")
		local EntryName        = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		local Expand           = Instance.new("TextButton")
		local Icon             = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		local Icon_2           = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		local PlayersFrame     = Instance.new("Frame")
		local UIListLayout     = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
		local PlaceHolderEntry = Instance.new("TextButton")
		local Indent_2         = Instance.new("Frame")
		local EntryName_2      = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		local Expand_2         = Instance.new("TextButton")
		local Icon_3           = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

		List.Name                 = "List"
		List.Parent               = content
		List.BackgroundColor3     = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		List.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		List.BorderColor3         = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
		List.BorderSizePixel      = 0
		List.Size                 = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)

		PlayerData.Name               = "PlayerData"
		PlayerData.Parent             = List
		PlayerData.BackgroundColor3   = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
		PlayerData.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		PlayerData.BorderColor3       = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
		PlayerData.Size               = UDim2.new(0, 205, 0, 20)
		PlayerData.AutoButtonColor    = false
		PlayerData.Font               = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		PlayerData.Text               = ""
		PlayerData.TextSize           = 14

		Indent.Name               = "Indent"
		Indent.Parent             = PlayerData
		Indent.BackgroundColor3   = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
		Indent.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		Indent.BorderColor3       = Color3.fromRGB(86, 125, 188)
		Indent.BorderSizePixel    = 0
		Indent.Position           = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 0)
		Indent.Size               = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)

		EntryName.Name               = "EntryName"
		EntryName.Parent             = Indent
		EntryName.BackgroundColor3   = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		EntryName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		EntryName.Position           = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 0)
		EntryName.Size               = UDim2.new(1, -26, 1, 0)
		EntryName.Font               = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		EntryName.Text               = "PlayerData"
		EntryName.TextColor3         = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
		EntryName.TextSize           = 14
		EntryName.TextXAlignment     = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

		Expand.Name               = "Expand"

		Expand.ClipsDescendants   = true
		Expand.Position           = UDim2.new(0, -20, 0, 0)

		EntryName_2.Size               = UDim2.new(1, -26, 1, 0)

		EntryName_2.Text               = "Camera"
		EntryName_2.TextColor3         = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
		EntryName_2.TextSize           = 14
		EntryName_2.TextXAlignment     = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

		Expand_2.Name               = "Expand"
		Expand_2.Parent             = Indent_2
		Expand_2.BackgroundColor3   = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		Expand_2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		Expand_2.ClipsDescendants   = true
		Expand_2.Position           = UDim2.new(0, -20, 0, 0)
		Expand_2.Size               = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
		Expand_2.Font               = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		Expand_2.Text               = ""
		Expand_2.TextSize           = 14

		Icon_3.Name               = "Icon"
		Icon_3.Parent             = Indent_2
		Icon_3.BackgroundColor3   = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		Icon_3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		Icon_3.Position           = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 1)
		Icon_3.Size               = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
		Icon_3.ScaleType          = Enum.ScaleType.Crop

		-- ── Lógica do Packages ──────────────
		local Players       = game:GetService("Players")
		local ListedPlayers = {}

		local PlayerDataButton = PlayerData

		local SimpleHash = function(tbl)
			local str = ""
			for k,v in pairs(tbl) do
				str = str .. tostring(k) .. tostring(v)
			end
			return tostring(str):sub(1,32)
		end

		local SetEntryColor = function(entry)
			local color = Color3.fromRGB(13, 104, 202)
			for i,ent in pairs(List:GetDescendants()) do
				if ent:IsA("Frame") then
					if ent.BackgroundColor3 == color then
						ent.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
						ent.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					end
				end
			end
			if entry:IsA("TextButton") then
				if entry:FindFirstChild("Indent") then
					entry.Indent.BackgroundColor3 = color
					entry.Indent.BackgroundTransparency = 0
				end
			end
		end

		PlayerDataButton.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
			SetEntryColor(PlayerDataButton)
		end)

		local hash = SimpleHash(ListedPlayers)

		PlayerDataButton.Indent.Expand.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
			if PlayersFrame.Visible then
				PlayersFrame.Visible = false
				PlayerDataButton.Indent.Expand.Rotation = 0
			else
				PlayersFrame.Visible = true
				PlayerDataButton.Indent.Expand.Rotation = 90
			end
		end)

		Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
			if not table.find(ListedPlayers, plr.Name) then return end
			table.remove(ListedPlayers, table.find(ListedPlayers, plr.Name))
		end)
		Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
			if table.find(ListedPlayers, plr.Name) then return end
			table.insert(ListedPlayers, plr.Name)
		end)

		for i,plr in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if not table.find(ListedPlayers, plr.Name) then
				table.insert(ListedPlayers, plr.Name)
			end
		end

		hash = ""

		game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function(delta)
			if SimpleHash(ListedPlayers) == hash then return end
			hash = SimpleHash(ListedPlayers)

			for i,frames in pairs(PlayersFrame:GetDescendants()) do
				if frames.Name == "EntryFrameData" then
					frames:Destroy()
				end
			end

			for i,plr in pairs(ListedPlayers) do
				local new = PlaceHolderEntry:Clone()
				new.Name = "EntryFrameData"
				new.Visible = true
				new.Parent = PlayersFrame
				new.Indent.EntryName.Text = plr
				new.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
					if new:IsA("TextButton") then
						if new:FindFirstChild("Indent") then
							if new.Indent.BackgroundColor3 == Color3.fromRGB(13, 104, 202) then
								local target = Players:FindFirstChild(plr)
								if target then DisplayValueEditor(target) end
							end
						end
					end
					SetEntryColor(new)
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	return DebugExplorer
end
return {InitDeps = InitDeps, InitAfterMain = InitAfterMain, Main = MainFunc}
end,

		TimeBox.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 36)
		TimeBox.Text = ""
		TimeBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
		TimeBox.Parent = PlayerFrame

		CornerTime.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
		CornerTime.Parent = TimeBox

		-- Play Button (Player)
		local PlayBtnPlayer = Instance.new("TextButton")
		PlayBtnPlayer.Name = "PlayBtnPlayer"
		PlayBtnPlayer.BorderSizePixel = 0
		PlayBtnPlayer.TextSize = 17
		PlayBtnPlayer.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		PlayBtnPlayer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 127, 206)
		PlayBtnPlayer.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
		PlayBtnPlayer.Size = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 28)
		PlayBtnPlayer.Text = "Play"
		PlayBtnPlayer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 322)
		PlayBtnPlayer.Parent = PlayerFrame

		local CornerPlayPlayer = Instance.new("UICorner")
		CornerPlayPlayer.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
		CornerPlayPlayer.Parent = PlayBtnPlayer

		-- Back Button
		local BackBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
		BackBtn.Name = "BackBtn"
		BackBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		BackBtn.TextSize = 17
		BackBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		BackBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 127, 206)
		BackBtn.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
		BackBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 28)
		BackBtn.Text = "Back to Home"
		BackBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 358)
		BackBtn.Parent = PlayerFrame

		local CornerBack = Instance.new("UICorner")
		CornerBack.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
		CornerBack.Parent = BackBtn

		-- ============================================================
		-- FUNCIONALIDADE
		-- ============================================================

		local currentSound = nil

		PlayBtnHome.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			HomeFrame.Visible = false
			PlayerFrame.Visible = true
		end)

		BackBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			PlayerFrame.Visible = false
			HomeFrame.Visible = true
		end)

		PlayBtnPlayer.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			local soundId = IDBox.Text
			if soundId == "" then return end

			if tonumber(soundId) then
				soundId = "rbxassetid://" .. soundId
			end

			if currentSound then
				currentSound:Stop()
				currentSound:Destroy()
			end

			currentSound = Instance.new("Sound")
			currentSound.SoundId = soundId
			currentSound.Parent = SoundService

			local pitch = tonumber(PitchBox.Text)
			if pitch then
				currentSound.PlaybackSpeed = pitch
			end

			local volume = tonumber(VolumeBox.Text)
			if volume then
				currentSound.Volume = math.clamp(volume, 0, 10)
			end

			local timePos = tonumber(TimeBox.Text)
			if timePos then
				currentSound.TimePosition = timePos
			end

			currentSound:Play()

			local originalText = PlayBtnPlayer.Text
			PlayBtnPlayer.Text = "Playing..."
			task.delay(1, function()
				if PlayBtnPlayer then
					PlayBtnPlayer.Text = originalText
				end
			end)
		end)

		DexSoundPlayer.Window = window
	end

	return DexSoundPlayer
end
return {InitDeps = initDeps, InitAfterMain = initAfterMain, Main = main}
end

}

-- Vars
local Settings = DefaultSettings or {}

local plr = service.Players.LocalPlayer or service.Players.PlayerAdded:wait()

local create = function(data)
	local insts = {}
	for i,v in pairs(data) do insts[v[1]] = Instance.new(v[2]) end

	for _,v in pairs(data) do
		for prop,val in pairs(v[3]) do
			if type(val) == "table" then
				insts[v[1]][prop] = insts[val[1]]
			else
				insts[v[1]][prop] = val
			end
		end
	end

	return insts[1]
end

local createSimple = function(class,props)
	local inst = Instance.new(class)
	for i,v in next,props do
		inst[i] = v
	end
	return inst
end

		end
	end

		--[[if Main.Elevated then -- If you don't have filesystem api then ur outta luck tbh

			Main.AppControls[name] = control

			local moduleData = control.Main()
			Apps[name] = moduleData

		else
			local module = script:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild(name, 2)
			if not module then Main.Error("CANNOT FIND MODULE " .. name) end

			local control = require(module)
			Main.AppControls[name] = control

			local moduleData = control.Main()

		Main.AppControls[name] = control

	Main.InitEnv = function()
		setmetatable(env, {__newindex = function(self, name, func)
			if not func then Main.MissingEnv[#Main.MissingEnv + 1] = name return end
			rawset(self, name, func)
		end})

		-- file
		env.readfile = missing("function", readfile)
		env.writefile = missing("function", writefile)
		env.appendfile = missing("function", appendfile)
		env.makefolder = missing("function", makefolder)
		env.listfiles = missing("function", listfiles)
		env.isfile = missing("function", isfile)
		env.isfolder = missing("function", isfolder)
		env.loadfile = missing("function", loadfile)
		env.movefileas = missing("function", movefileas)
		local Serializers = {}
		env.saveinstance = missing("function", saveinstance) or function(obj, name, options)
			local method = Settings.SaveInstance and Settings.SaveInstance.Method or "DexSerializer"
			
			if method == "UniversalSynSaveInstance" then
				if not Serializers.Syn then
					local success, res = pcall(function()
						return loadstring(oldgame:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/luau/SynSaveInstance/main/saveinstance.luau"))()
					end)
					if success and type(res) == "function" then
						Serializers.Syn = res
					else
						return warn("Failed to load UniversalSynSaveInstance")
					end
				end
				
				options = options or {}
				options.Object = type(obj) == "table" and obj[1] or obj -- SynSaveInstance doesn't natively support tables of objects
				if type(name) == "string" then
					options.FilePath = name
				end
				return Serializers.Syn(options)
			else
				if not Serializers.Dex then
					local success, res = pcall(function()
						return loadstring(oldgame:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Tesker-103/DexRecontinued/refs/heads/main/Serializer.lua"))()
					end)
					if success and type(res) == "table" and res.Save then
						pcall(res.Init)
						Serializers.Dex = res
					else
						return warn("Failed to load DexSerializer")
					end
				end
				
				return Serializers.Dex.Save(obj, name, options)
			end
		end
		env.parsefile = function(name)
			return tostring(name):gsub("[*\\?:<>|]+", ""):sub(1, 175)
		end

		-- debug
		env.getupvalues = missing("function", (debug and debug.getupvalues) or getupvalues or getupvals)
		env.setupvalues = missing("function", (debug and debug.setupvalues) or setupvalues or setupvals)
		env.setconstants = missing("function", (debug and debug.setconstants) or setconstants or setconsts)
		env.setconstant = missing("function", (debug and debug.setconstant) or setconstant or setconst)

		env.getconstants = missing("function", (debug and debug.getconstants) or getconstants or getconsts)
		env.getconnections = missing("function", getconnections or get_connections or get_conns)
		env.getinfo = missing("function", (debug and (debug.getinfo or debug.info)) or getinfo)

		local hookfunction = env.hookfunction
if oth and oth.hook then
    local old = hookfunction
    env.hookfunction = function(f1, f2)
        if debug.info(f1, "s") == "[C]" then
            return oth.hook(f1, f2)
        else
            return old(f1, f2)
        end
    end
end
		env.newcclosure = missing("function", newcclosure)
		env.restorefunction = missing("function", restorefunction)
		--env.base64encode = missing("function", crypt and crypt.base64 and crypt.base64.encode)
		env.getscriptbytecode = missing("function", getscriptbytecode)

		env.gethiddenproperty = missing("function", gethiddenproperty)

		-- other
		--env.setfflag = missing("function", setfflag)

		-- other
		env.request = (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or http_request or (fluxus and fluxus.request) or request
		
		env.isdecompile = function()
			return typeof(decompile) == "function" or typeof(getscriptbytecode) == "function" or false
		end
		
		-- DECOMPILERS
		local AdvancedDecompilerCache
		pcall(function()
			AdvancedDecompilerCache = loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AZYsGithub/Advanced-Decompiler-V3/refs/heads/main/init.lua"))()
		end)
		
		local function base64Encode(data)
			local encoder = (crypt and crypt.base64encode) or (crypt and crypt.base64 and crypt.base64.encode) or (base64_encode)
			if encoder then
				local s, res = pcall(encoder, data)
				if s and res then return res end
			end
			local b = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/'
			return ((data:gsub('.', function(x)
				local r,byte = '',x:byte()
				for i=8,1,-1 do r = r .. (byte % 2^i - byte % 2^(i-1) > 0 and '1' or '0') end
				return r
			end)..'0000'):gsub('%d%d%d?%d?%d?%d?', function(x)
				if #x < 6 then return '' end
				local c = 0
				for i=1,6 do c = c + (x:sub(i,i) == '1' and 2^(6-i) or 0) end
				return b:sub(c+1,c+1)
			end)..({ '', '==', '=' })[#data % 3 + 1])
		end

		local konstant_last_call = 0
		local function KonstantDec(...)
			local API = "http://api.plusgiant5.com"
			local request = env.request
			local function call(konstantType, scriptPath)
				local success, bytecode = pcall(env.getscriptbytecode, scriptPath)
				if (not success) then return "-- Failed to get script bytecode\n" end
				local time_elapsed = os.clock() - konstant_last_call
				if time_elapsed <= .5 then task.wait(.5 - time_elapsed) end
				local httpResult = env.request({
					Url = API .. konstantType, Body = bytecode, Method = "POST", Headers = {["Content-Type"] = "text/plain"}
				})
				konstant_last_call = os.clock()
				if (httpResult.StatusCode ~= 200) then return "-- Error occurred while requesting Konstant API\n" else return httpResult.Body end
			end
			return call("/konstant/decompile", ...)
		end

		local ADDec = AdvancedDecompilerCache or function() return "Failed to load Advanced Decompiler" end

		local lua_expert_last = 0
		local function LuaExpertDec(scr)
			if not env.getscriptbytecode then return "-- exploit does not support getscriptbytecode." end
			local ok, bytecode = pcall(env.getscriptbytecode, scr)
			if not ok then return "-- failed to read script bytecode\n--[[\n" .. tostring(bytecode) .. "\n--]]" end

			local elapsed = os.clock() - lua_expert_last
			if elapsed < 0.12 then task.wait(0.12 - elapsed) end

			local res = env.request({
				Url = "https://api.lua.expert/decompile",
				Method = "POST",
				Headers = { ["content-type"] = "application/json" },
				Body = service.HttpService:JSONEncode({ script = base64Encode(bytecode) })
			})
			lua_expert_last = os.clock()

			if not res or res.StatusCode ~= 200 then
				return "-- api request error\n--[[\n" .. (res and res.Body or "no response") .. "\n--]]"
			end
			return res.Body
		end

		local function ShinyDec(script_instance)
			if typeof(crypt) ~= "table" then return "-- 'crypt' library is missing!" end
			local port = Settings.Decompiler and Settings.Decompiler.ShinyDecompilerPort or 3000
			local success, result = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet("http://127.0.0.1:"..tostring(port)) end)
			if not success then return "-- Shiny decompiler is not active or port is wrong!" end
			local bytecode = getscriptbytecode(script_instance)
			local encoded = base64Encode(bytecode)
			return env.request({
				Url = "http://127.0.0.1:"..tostring(port).."/luau/decompile", Method = "POST", Body = encoded
			}).Body
		end

		local function SabreDec(scr, mode)
			if not env.getscriptbytecode then return "-- Sabre: getscriptbytecode is not supported" end
			local ok, bytecode = pcall(env.getscriptbytecode, scr)
			if not ok or not bytecode or bytecode == "" then
				return "-- Sabre: Failed to get script bytecode"
			end

			local body = service.HttpService:JSONEncode({
				target = "luau",
				mode = mode or "decompile", -- Defaults to decompile if not specified
				bytecodeBase64 = base64Encode(bytecode),
			})

			local res = env.request({
				Url = "https://www.decompiler.lol/api/decompile",
				Method = "POST",
				Headers = {
					["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
				},
				Body = body,
			})

			if not res or not res.Body then
				return "-- Sabre: No response body from server"
			end

			local ok_json, parsed = pcall(function()
				return service.HttpService:JSONDecode(res.Body)
			end)

			if not ok_json or type(parsed) ~= "table" then
				return "-- Sabre: Invalid JSON response: " .. tostring(res.Body):sub(1, 200)
			end

			if not parsed.ok then
				return "-- Sabre: " .. string.format("[%s] %s", parsed.stage or "?", parsed.error or "unknown error")
			end

			return parsed.output or "-- Sabre: Empty output"
		end

		local function DisassemblyDec(scr)
			return SabreDec(scr, "disasm")
		end

		local function X2125Dec(scr)
			if not env.getscriptbytecode then return "-- x2125: getscriptbytecode is not supported" end
			local ok, bytecode = pcall(env.getscriptbytecode, scr)
			if not ok or not bytecode or bytecode == "" then
				return "-- x2125: Failed to get script bytecode"
			end

			local res = env.request({
				Url = "https://x2125.xyz/decompile",
				Method = "POST",
				Headers = {
					["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
				},
				Body = service.HttpService:JSONEncode({
					script = base64Encode(bytecode),
					options = {
						renamingType = "INFER",
						upvalueComment = true,
						lineDefinedComment = true,
					},
				}),
			})

			if not res or not res.Body then
				return "-- x2125: No response body"
			end

			if res.StatusCode == 200 then
				local ok_json, decoded = pcall(function()
					return service.HttpService:JSONDecode(res.Body)
				end)
				if ok_json and type(decoded) == "table" and type(decoded.data) == "string" then
					return decoded.data
				end
				return "-- x2125: Invalid server response structure"
			end

			local errorMsg = "HTTP " .. tostring(res.StatusCode)
			if type(res.Body) == "string" and res.Body ~= "" then
				local ok_json, edec = pcall(function()
					return service.HttpService:JSONDecode(res.Body)
				end)
				if ok_json and type(edec) == "table" and type(edec.error) == "string" then
					errorMsg = edec.error
				else
					errorMsg = res.Body
				end
			end

			return "-- x2125 Error: " .. errorMsg
		end

		local function BytefallDec(scr)
			if not env.getscriptbytecode then return "-- ByteFall: getscriptbytecode is not supported" end
			local ok, bytecode = pcall(env.getscriptbytecode, scr)
			if not ok or not bytecode or bytecode == "" then
				return "-- ByteFall: getscriptbytecode returned empty or failed"
			end

			local body = service.HttpService:JSONEncode({
				script = base64Encode(bytecode)
			})

			local res = env.request({
				Url = "https://beta.bytefall.dev/decompile",
				Method = "POST",
				Headers = {
					["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
				},
				Body = body,
			})

			if not res or not res.Body then
				return "-- ByteFall: No response body"
			end

			local code = res.StatusCode or 0
			if code == 200 then
				return res.Body
			elseif code == 400 then
				return "-- ByteFall (400 bad request): " .. tostring(res.Body)
			elseif code == 500 then
				return "-- ByteFall (500 decompilation failed): " .. tostring(res.Body)
			elseif code == 404 then
				return "-- ByteFall (404 not found): wrong endpoint?"
			else
				return "-- ByteFall (HTTP " .. tostring(code) .. "): " .. tostring(res.Body)
			end
		end

		env.decompile = function(...)
			if typeof(decompile) == "function" and (not Settings.Decompiler or Settings.Decompiler.PreferDecompilerFallback == false) then
				return decompile(...)
			elseif typeof(getscriptbytecode) == "function" then
				local fallbackMode = Settings.Decompiler and Settings.Decompiler.DecompilerFallback or "Konstant"
				if fallbackMode == "Konstant" then return KonstantDec(...)
				elseif fallbackMode == "AdvancedDecompiler" then return ADDec(...)
				elseif fallbackMode == "Shiny" then return ShinyDec(...)
				elseif fallbackMode == "LuaExpert" then return LuaExpertDec(...)
				elseif fallbackMode == "Sabre" then return SabreDec(...)
				elseif fallbackMode == "x2125" then return X2125Dec(...)
				elseif fallbackMode == "Bytefall" then return BytefallDec(...)
				elseif fallbackMode == "Disassembly" then return DisassemblyDec(...) -- New routing mapping
				end
			end
		end

		env.isViableDecompileScript = function(obj)
			if obj:IsA("ModuleScript") then
				return true
			elseif obj:IsA("LocalScript") and (obj.RunContext == Enum.RunContext.Client or obj.RunContext == Enum.RunContext.Legacy) then
				return true
			elseif obj:IsA("Script") and obj.RunContext == Enum.RunContext.Client then
				return true
			end
			return false
		end
		env.protectgui = missing("function", protect_gui or (syn and syn.protect_gui))
		env.gethui = missing("function", gethui or get_hidden_gui)
		env.setclipboard = missing("function", setclipboard or toclipboard or set_clipboard or (Clipboard and Clipboard.set))
		env.getnilinstances = missing("function", getnilinstances or get_nil_instances)
		env.getprotos = missing("function", debug.getprotos)
		env.getsenv = missing("function", getsenv)
		env.iscclosure = missing("function", iscclosure)
		env.isourclosure = missing("function", isexecutorclosure or isourclosure)
		env.getfunctionhash = missing("function", getfunctionhash)
		env.loadstring = missing("function", loadstring)
		env.getloadedmodules = missing("function", getloadedmodules)

		env.tostring = function(...)
        local args = {...}
        local getrawmetatable = getrawmetatable or debug.getmetatable or getmetatable

        -- since varargs will automatically convert last args that are nil to nothing, we can just make them "nil" (not using table.pack)
        if #args < select("#", ...) then
            for i = #args+1, select("#",...) do
                args[i] = "nil"
            end
        end

        for i, v in pairs(args) do
            if (typeof(v) == "table" or typeof(v) == "userdata") and getrawmetatable(v) and rawget(getrawmetatable(v), "__tostring") then
                local mt = getrawmetatable(v)
                local func = rawget(mt, "__tostring")
                rawset(mt, "__tostring", nil)
                args[i] = tostring(v)
                rawset(mt, "__tostring", func)
            else
                args[i] = tostring(v)
            end
        end

        return unpack(args)
    end

		env.executor = (function()
			local identifyexec = identifyexecutor or getexecutorname or whatexecutor
			local success, name = pcall(function()
				return tostring(identifyexec())
			end)
			return success and name or "Your executor"
		end)()

		Main.GuiHolder = Main.Elevated and service.CoreGui or plr:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("PlayerGui")

		setmetatable(env, nil)
	end

	local function serialize(val)
		if typeof(val) == "Color3" then
			return {R = val.R, G = val.G, B = val.B}
		else
			return val
		end
	end
	
	local function deserialize(val)
		if typeof(val) == "table" and val.R and val.G and val.B then
			return Color3.new(val.R, val.G, val.B)
		else
			return val
		end
	end
	
	Main.ExportSettings = function()
		local rawData = Settings or DefaultSettings
		local function recur(tbl)
			local newTbl = {}
			for i, v in pairs(tbl) do
				if typeof(v) == "table" then
					newTbl[i] = recur(v)
				else
					newTbl[i] = serialize(v)
				end
			end
			return newTbl
		end
		local s, json = pcall(service.HttpService.JSONEncode, service.HttpService, recur(rawData))
		if s and json then return json end
	end

	Main.LoadSettings = function()
		local Success, Data = pcall(env.readfile or error, "DEX_REContinued/DexRESettings.json")
		Main.ResetSettings()
		
		if Success and Data and Data ~= "" then
			local DecodeSuccess, Decoded = pcall(service.HttpService.JSONDecode, service.HttpService, Data)
			if DecodeSuccess and Decoded then
				
				local function Merge(Target, Source)
				for Key, Value in pairs(Source) do
				if typeof(Value) == "table" then
						if Value.R and Value.G and Value.B then
						Target[Key] = Color3.new(Value.R, Value.G, Value.B)
						else
						if type(Target[Key]) ~= "table" then
						Target[Key] = {}
						end
						Merge(Target[Key], Value)
						end
						else
						Target[Key] = Value
						end
					end
				end
				Merge(Settings, Decoded)
			end
		end

		-- Always use the classic (Old) explorer icons
		Settings.ClassIcon = "Old"
	end

	Main.SaveCurrentSettings = function()
		if env.writefile then
			env.writefile("DEX_REContinued/DexRESettings.json", Main.ExportSettings())
		end
	end

	Main.ResetSettings = function()
		local function recur(t,res)
			for set,val in pairs(t) do
				if type(val) == "table" and val._Recurse then
					if type(res[set]) ~= "table" then
						res[set] = {}
					end
					recur(val,res[set])
				else
					res[set] = val
				end
			end
			return res
		end
		recur(DefaultSettings,Settings)
	end

	Main.UpdateThemeColor = function(oldColor, newColor)
		if oldColor == newColor then return end
		
		-- Use math.abs for Color3 comparison due to floating point inaccuracies
		local function cmp(c1, c2)
			return math.abs(c1.R - c2.R) < 0.01 and math.abs(c1.G - c2.G) < 0.01 and math.abs(c1.B - c2.B) < 0.01
		end
		
		local function scan(inst)
			pcall(function()
				if inst:IsA("GuiObject") then
					if cmp(inst.BackgroundColor3, oldColor) then inst.BackgroundColor3 = newColor end
					if cmp(inst.BorderColor3, oldColor) then inst.BorderColor3 = newColor end
					if inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextBox") or inst:IsA("TextButton") then
						if cmp(inst.TextColor3, oldColor) then inst.TextColor3 = newColor end
						if inst:IsA("TextBox") and cmp(inst.PlaceholderColor3, oldColor) then inst.PlaceholderColor3 = newColor end
					end
					if inst:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
						if cmp(inst.ScrollBarImageColor3, oldColor) then inst.ScrollBarImageColor3 = newColor end
					end
				elseif inst:IsA("UIStroke") then
					if cmp(inst.Color, oldColor) then inst.Color = newColor end
				end
			end)
			for _, child in next, inst:GetChildren() do
				scan(child)
			end
		end
		
		if Main.GuiHolder then
			for _, child in next, Main.GuiHolder:GetChildren() do
				scan(child)
			end
		end
	end

	local function jsonDecode(str)
		local suc, res = pcall(service.HttpService.JSONDecode, service.HttpService, str)
		return suc and res or suc
	end

	Main.FetchAPI = function()
		local api,rawAPI
		if Main.Elevated then
			if Main.LocalDepsUpToDate() then
				local localAPI = Lib.ReadFile("DEX_REContinued/rbx_api.dat")
				if localAPI then 
					rawAPI = localAPI
				else
					Main.DepsVersionData[1] = ""
				end
			end
			rawAPI = rawAPI or oldgame:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/MaximumADHD/Roblox-Client-Tracker/refs/heads/roblox/Full-API-Dump.json")
		else
			if script:FindFirstChild("API") then
				rawAPI = require(script.API)
			else
				error("NO API EXISTS")
			end
		end
		Main.RawAPI = rawAPI
		api = jsonDecode(rawAPI)

		-- backup for kaboom
		if not api then
			rawAPI = oldgame:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/MaximumADHD/Roblox-Client-Tracker/refs/heads/roblox/Full-API-Dump.json")
			Main.RawAPI = rawAPI
			api = jsonDecode(rawAPI)
		end

		local classes,enums = {},{}
		local categoryOrder,seenCategories = {},{}

		local function insertAbove(t,item,aboveItem)
			local findPos = table.find(t,item)
			if not findPos then return end
			table.remove(t,findPos)

			local pos = table.find(t,aboveItem)
			if not pos then return end
			table.insert(t,pos,item)
		end

		for _,class in pairs(api.Classes) do
			local newClass = {}
			newClass.Name = class.Name
			newClass.Superclass = class.Superclass
			newClass.Properties = {}
			newClass.Functions = {}
			newClass.Events = {}
			newClass.Callbacks = {}
			newClass.Tags = {}

			if class.Tags then 
				for _,tag in pairs(class.Tags) do 
					if type(tag) == "string" then newClass.Tags[tag] = true end 
				end 
			end
			
			for __,member in pairs(class.Members) do
				local newMember = {}
				newMember.Name = member.Name
				newMember.Class = class.Name
				newMember.Security = member.Security
				newMember.Tags = {}
				
				if member.Tags then 
					for _,tag in pairs(member.Tags) do 
						if type(tag) == "string" then newMember.Tags[tag] = true end 
					end 
				end

				local mType = member.MemberType
				if mType == "Property" then
					local propCategory = member.Category or "Other"
					propCategory = propCategory:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
					if not seenCategories[propCategory] then
						categoryOrder[#categoryOrder+1] = propCategory
						seenCategories[propCategory] = true
					end
					newMember.ValueType = member.ValueType
					newMember.Category = propCategory
					newMember.Serialization = member.Serialization
					table.insert(newClass.Properties,newMember)
				elseif mType == "Function" then
					newMember.Parameters = {}
					newMember.ReturnType = member.ReturnType and member.ReturnType.Name or "void"
					if member.Parameters then
						for c,param in pairs(member.Parameters) do
							table.insert(newMember.Parameters,{Name = param.Name, Type = param.Type and param.Type.Name or "any"})
						end
					end
					table.insert(newClass.Functions,newMember)
				elseif mType == "Event" then
					newMember.Parameters = {}
					if member.Parameters then
						for c,param in pairs(member.Parameters) do
							table.insert(newMember.Parameters,{Name = param.Name, Type = param.Type and param.Type.Name or "any"})
						end
					end
					table.insert(newClass.Events,newMember)
				end
			end

			classes[class.Name] = newClass
		end

		for _,class in pairs(classes) do
			class.Superclass = classes[class.Superclass]
		end

		for _,enum in pairs(api.Enums) do
			local newEnum = {}
			newEnum.Name = enum.Name
			newEnum.Items = {}
			newEnum.Tags = {}

			if enum.Tags then for c,tag in pairs(enum.Tags) do newEnum.Tags[tag] = true end end
			for __,item in pairs(enum.Items) do
				local newItem = {}
				newItem.Name = item.Name
				newItem.Value = item.Value
				table.insert(newEnum.Items,newItem)
			end

			enums[enum.Name] = newEnum
		end

		local function getMember(class,member)
			if not classes[class] or not classes[class][member] then return end
			local result = {}

			local currentClass = classes[class]
			while currentClass do
				for _,entry in pairs(currentClass[member]) do
					result[#result+1] = entry
				end
				currentClass = currentClass.Superclass
			end

			table.sort(result,function(a,b) return a.Name < b.Name end)
			return result
		end

		insertAbove(categoryOrder,"Behavior","Tuning")
		insertAbove(categoryOrder,"Appearance","Data")
		insertAbove(categoryOrder,"Attachments","Axes")
		insertAbove(categoryOrder,"Cylinder","Slider")
		insertAbove(categoryOrder,"Localization","Jump Settings")
		insertAbove(categoryOrder,"Surface","Motion")
		insertAbove(categoryOrder,"Surface Inputs","Surface")
		insertAbove(categoryOrder,"Part","Surface Inputs")
		insertAbove(categoryOrder,"Assembly","Surface Inputs")
		insertAbove(categoryOrder,"Character","Controls")
		categoryOrder[#categoryOrder+1] = "Unscriptable"
		categoryOrder[#categoryOrder+1] = "Attributes"
		categoryOrder[#categoryOrder+1] = "Tags"

		local categoryOrderMap = {}
		for i = 1,#categoryOrder do
			categoryOrderMap[categoryOrder[i]] = i
		end

		return {
			Classes = classes,
			Enums = enums,
			CategoryOrder = categoryOrderMap,
			GetMember = getMember
		}
	end

	Main.FetchRMD = function()
		local rawXML
		if Main.Elevated then
			if Main.LocalDepsUpToDate() then
				local localRMD = Lib.ReadFile("DEX_REContinued/rbx_rmd.dat")
				if localRMD then 
					rawXML = localRMD
				else
					Main.DepsVersionData[1] = ""
				end
			end
			rawXML = rawXML or oldgame:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/CloneTrooper1019/Roblox-Client-Tracker/roblox/ReflectionMetadata.xml")
		else
			if script:FindFirstChild("RMD") then
				rawXML = require(script.RMD)
			else
				error("NO RMD EXISTS")
			end
		end
		Main.RawRMD = rawXML
		local parsed = Lib.ParseXML(rawXML)
		local classList = parsed.children[1].children[1].children
		local enumList = parsed.children[1].children[2].children
		local propertyOrders = {}

		local classes,enums = {},{}
		for _,class in pairs(classList) do
			local className = ""
			for _,child in pairs(class.children) do
				if child.tag == "Properties" then
					local data = {Properties = {}, Functions = {}}
					local props = child.children
					for _,prop in pairs(props) do
						local name = prop.attrs.name
						name = name:sub(1,1):upper()..name:sub(2)
						data[name] = prop.children[1].text
					end
					className = data.Name
					classes[className] = data
				elseif child.attrs.class == "ReflectionMetadataProperties" then
					local members = child.children
					for _,member in pairs(members) do
						if member.attrs.class == "ReflectionMetadataMember" then
							local data = {}
							if member.children[1].tag == "Properties" then
								local props = member.children[1].children
								for _,prop in pairs(props) do
									if prop.attrs then
										local name = prop.attrs.name
										name = name:sub(1,1):upper()..name:sub(2)
										data[name] = prop.children[1].text
									end
								end
								if data.PropertyOrder then
									local orders = propertyOrders[className]
									if not orders then orders = {} propertyOrders[className] = orders end
									orders[data.Name] = tonumber(data.PropertyOrder)
								end
								classes[className].Properties[data.Name] = data
							end
						end
					end
				elseif child.attrs.class == "ReflectionMetadataFunctions" then
					local members = child.children
					for _,member in pairs(members) do
						if member.attrs.class == "ReflectionMetadataMember" then
							local data = {}
							if member.children[1].tag == "Properties" then
								local props = member.children[1].children
								for _,prop in pairs(props) do
									if prop.attrs then
										local name = prop.attrs.name
										name = name:sub(1,1):upper()..name:sub(2)
										data[name] = prop.children[1].text
									end
								end
								classes[className].Functions[data.Name] = data
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		for _,enum in pairs(enumList) do
			local enumName = ""
			for _,child in pairs(enum.children) do
				if child.tag == "Properties" then
					local data = {Items = {}}
					local props = child.children
					for _,prop in pairs(props) do
						local name = prop.attrs.name
						name = name:sub(1,1):upper()..name:sub(2)
						data[name] = prop.children[1].text
					end
					enumName = data.Name
					enums[enumName] = data
				elseif child.attrs.class == "ReflectionMetadataEnumItem" then
					local data = {}
					if child.children[1].tag == "Properties" then
						local props = child.children[1].children
						for _,prop in pairs(props) do
							local name = prop.attrs.name
							name = name:sub(1,1):upper()..name:sub(2)
							data[name] = prop.children[1].text
						end
						enums[enumName].Items[data.Name] = data
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return {Classes = classes, Enums = enums, PropertyOrders = propertyOrders}
	end

	Main.ShowGui = function(gui)
		if env.gethui then
			gui.Parent = env.gethui()
		elseif env.protectgui then
			env.protectgui(gui)
			gui.Parent = Main.GuiHolder
		else
			gui.Parent = Main.GuiHolder
		end
	end
